# corpus-n provenance (#1045)

24 unchanged public application/regression decks. Licence policy is redistributable with recorded terms (CHARTER); the supervisor explicitly allowed GPL-3.0-or-later Xyce inputs. BSD ngspice inputs and GPL Xyce inputs remain separate from MIT packages. SHA-256 dedupe against every existing corpus file, plus terminal-newline variants and earlier candidates. No adaptation or spice-ts-success filter. Uniform existing harness metrics, no tolerance edits; every spice-ts loss published. No aggregate refresh.

- ngspice: pinned revision `a80f6e3e95d51534905b1f23410a951802666656`, BSD-3-Clause. [Licence notice](https://github.com/imr/ngspice/blob/a80f6e3e95d51534905b1f23410a951802666656/COPYING). COPYING explicitly adopts Modified BSD for all source, test and example files except listed paths; none of the selected example/test paths is an exception. The licensing document itself is CC-BY-SA-4.0 and is retained unchanged with attribution via the pinned URL.
- xyce: pinned revision `7bb7e98f0ed3a81a7d1cf1d10b68592107ed40b2`, GPL-3.0-or-later. [Licence notice](https://github.com/Xyce/Xyce_Regression/blob/7bb7e98f0ed3a81a7d1cf1d10b68592107ed40b2/README.md). Repository README grants GPL version 3 or later. Selected decks have no separate notice or external dependencies. README and full GPL text retained unchanged; independent benchmark inputs, not package code. Supervisor confirmed this licence is allowed under CHARTER.

## Rejected and deferred candidates

All 100 static-eligible BSD decks were probed: 13 success, 85 failed/no reference, 2 unsupported. All 87 non-reference outcomes are retained in ngspice-probe.json. Xyce selection stops at 11 successful references; unprobed remainder is deferred, not silently called failures. Static rejections and all probed failures are retained below and in both survey/probe receipts. Other Xyce families are deferred. Vendor/LTspice and unlicensed textbook/ISCAS material was not vendored.
